### Diagnosis + rationale
- **#143 premise correction (hard rule):** Auditor's live verification that `workflows: write` is absent is factually true but the inference is invalid. `workflows` is NOT a valid `GITHUB_TOKEN` `permissions:` scope per GitHub's workflow-syntax reference (16 scopes only). Adding it cannot enable an App-token merge of workflow files; GitHub hard-blocks App tokens from writing workflows regardless of what `permissions:` claims (there is no such permission to grant). This was verified by the contents API this run (all four mutating workflows correctly carry no `workflows:`) and was the exact reason owner STOP on #141 halted the approach. The Auditor's proposed step 1 (add `workflows: write`) would leave the deadlock intact and violate the infra routing guard's fact ("there is no workflows:write permission to grant; the idea that such a permission could be added is invalid" - `.github/agents/maintainer.md:78`).
- **Compliant unblock path:** LAB.md:73 already states the PAT-backed contract correctly and PR #142 proved it (docs-only merge via App token succeeded; workflow-touching merges require PAT or owner click). The honest fix is a hardcoded PAT-backed merge step in `maintainer.yml` (and PAT handling for Lab Engineer pushes), exactly the "lab-built hardcoded PAT-backed merge step" noted as the second unblock path for #139 in STATE. This is exempt from freeze (infra reliability fix) and directly restores Mae's autonomous merge capability after a single bootstrap owner click on the lab's own workflow-touching PR.
- **Why `lab` on #143 (not `fix`/`continue` on #139):** PR #139's diff touches `.github/workflows/*`, so `.github/agents/maintainer.md:74` infra routing guard forbids `fix`/`continue` (they push via App token and would re-hit the 403). The reviewer workflow would also auto-rewrite such a misrouted decision to `lab`. Routing to the Lab Engineer via `lab` is the only compliant path.
- No action on PR #131: D-series exhausted, both gates green at pinned head, owner decision point open, freeze blocks merge regardless. No Builder/Architect/Research/Review/Test dispatch until owner rules MANIAC vs honest closure. No `ideate` (board frozen). No `auditor` (health board current). No `recover`. - No emergency revival: requires demonstrably failed/broken lab attempt PLUS halted production (`.maintainer/emergency.json` contract). Lab track is blocked but not halted via a failed hard-gate - the failure is the App-token workflow guard itself, which the `lab` dispatch is the compliant remedy for. No valid `lab_run` URL to cite for emergency nor is production fully halted (main stable, PR #131 parked cleanly at decision point). Correct ladder is `lab` first.
